 # @openfilm/desktop
 
+## 0.2.5
+
+### Patch Changes
+

FFmpeg 6 wrote ProRes 4444 whose transparency editors read as opaque, so rendered pages came into 剪映 and other editors on black.

+test("DeepSeek Harness is found inside its desktop app on a Mac, which carries the command, and from the app the person chooses", () => {
+  if (process.platform === 'win32') return;
+  const home = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'of-home-'));

+  const none = { home, env: { PATH: '' }, pathDirs: [], applicationDirs: [apps] };

+  const dsh = join(apps, 'DeepSeek Harness.app', 'Contents', 'Resources', 'runtime', 'cli', 'bin', 'dsh');
+  mkdirSync(join(dsh, '..'), { recursive: true });
+  writeFileSync(dsh, '#!/bin/sh', { mode: 0o755 });

+/**
+ * One file out of a zip in memory, by its name: found in the central directory, stored or deflated. A zip of
+ * programs under 4 GB, so no Zip64.
+ * @param {Buffer} zip @param {string} name
+ */
+export function unzipEntry(zip, name) {
+  const end = zip.lastIndexOf(Buffer.from([0x50, 0x4b, 0x05, 0x06]));
+  if (end < 0) throw new Error('The ffmpeg download is not a zip.');
+  let at = zip.readUInt32LE(end + 16);
+  for (let i = zip.readUInt16LE(end + 10); i > 0; i--) {
+    const method = zip.readUInt16LE(at + 10);
+    const size = zip.readUInt32LE(at + 20);
+    const nameLength = zip.readUInt16LE(at + 28);
+    const skip = nameLength + zip.readUInt16LE(at + 30) + zip.readUInt16LE(at + 32);
+    if (zip.toString('utf8', at + 46, at + 46 + nameLength) === name) {
+      const local = zip.readUInt32LE(at + 42);
+      const start = local + 30 + zip.readUInt16LE(local + 26) + zip.readUInt16LE(local + 28);
+      const data = zip.subarray(start, start + size);
+      if (method === 0) return Buffer.from(data);
+      if (method === 8) return inflateRawSync(data);
+      throw new Error(`${name} is compressed in a way this can't read (method ${method}).`);
+    }
+    at += 46 + skip;
+  }
+  throw new Error(`The ffmpeg download has no ${name}.`);
+}
+

packages/openfilm/src/browser-pool.mjs
@@ -0,0 +1,85 @@
+// Browsers kept for many renders: Studio's editing-project export draws page after page with the same ones, instead
+// of starting a browser for each page. A render borrows some (take) and gives them back (give); each page opens its
+// own tab in them and closes it after.
+import { launch } from './host.mjs';
+
+/**
+ * Pages a browser draws before it is closed and a new one started. A browser's memory measured flat over 20 pages
+ * (a new tab each), so this only bounds whatever a page might leave behind in it.
+ */
+export const PAGES_PER_BROWSER = 24;
+
+export class BrowserPool {
+  /** @type {import('playwright-core').Browser[]} */
+  #idle = [];
+  /** @type {Map<import('playwright-core').Browser, number>} pages each browser has drawn */
+  #drawn = new Map();
+  #closed = false;
+  /** @param {{ start?: () => Promise<import('playwright-core').Browser> }} [opts] how a browser is started (tests) */
+  constructor({ start = () => launch() } = {}) {
+    this.start = start;
+  }
+
+  /** How many browsers are open: lent and idle. */
+  get size() { return this.#drawn.size; }
+
+  /**
+   * `n` browsers: idle ones first (one that died while idle is dropped), the rest started now, at once.
+   * @param {number} n @returns {Promise<import('playwright-core').Browser[]>}
+   */
+  async take(n) {
+    if (this.#closed) throw new Error('cancelled');
+    /** @type {import('playwright-core').Browser[]} */
+    const out = [];
+    while (out.length < n && this.#idle.length) {
+      const b = /** @type {import('playwright-core').Browser} */ (this.#idle.pop());
+      if (b.isConnected()) out.push(b);
+      else this.#drawn.delete(b);
+    }
+    const started = await Promise.allSettled(Array.from({ length: n - out.length }, () => this.start()));
+    for (const s of started) if (s.status === 'fulfilled') { this.#drawn.set(s.value, 0); out.push(s.value); }
+    const failed = started.find((s) => s.status === 'rejected');
+    if (failed || this.#closed) {
+      await this.give(out);
+      throw failed ? /** @type {PromiseRejectedResult} */ (failed).reason : new Error('cancelled');
+    }
+    return out;
+  }
+
+  /**
+   * Browsers back, a page drawn with each. One that crashed, one the render could not finish with (`retire`: it may
+   * hang), and one that has drawn PAGES_PER_BROWSER pages are closed; a new one starts when next asked for.
+   * @param {import('playwright-core').Browser[]} browsers @param {{ retire?: boolean }} [opts]
+   */
+  async give(browsers, { retire = false } = {}) {
+    await Promise.allSettled(browsers.map(async (b) => {
+      const drawn = (this.#drawn.get(b) ?? 0) + 1;
+      if (this.#closed || retire || !b.isConnected() || drawn >= PAGES_PER_BROWSER) {
+        this.#drawn.delete(b);
+        await b.close();
+        return;
+      }
+      this.#drawn.set(b, drawn);
+      this.#idle.push(b);
+    }));
+  }
+
+  /** Every browser closed, lent or not; take fails from now on. */
+  async close() {
+    this.#closed = true;
+    const all = [...this.#drawn.keys()];
+    this.#drawn.clear();
+    this.#idle = [];
+    await Promise.allSettled(all.map((b) => b.close()));
+  }
+}
+
+/** What a browser that crashed, or a tab that stopped answering, says: drawn again in new browsers, it may work. */
+const BROWSER_FAILED = /browser drawing the page closed|target (page, context or browser has been )?closed|target crashed|page crashed|browser has been closed|browser (has )?disconnected|did not finish within|did not come within/i;
+
+/**
+ * Whether a render that failed is worth drawing again in new browsers: its browser crashed or stopped answering. A
+ * page's own error (a frame that throws, a file that is missing) or a loop in its code would fail again.
+ * @param {unknown} e
+ */
+export const browserFailed = (e) => e instanceof Error && BROWSER_FAILED.test(e.message) && !/still running code/.test(e.message);

packages/openfilm/src/browser-pool.test.mjs
@@ -0,0 +1,76 @@
+import { test } from 'node:test';
+import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
+import { BrowserPool, PAGES_PER_BROWSER, browserFailed } from './browser-pool.mjs';
+
+/** A stand-in browser: connected until it is closed or `crash()`ed. */
+function fakes() {
+  let n = 0;
+  const started = [];
+  const start = async () => {
+    const b = { id: ++n, connected: true, closed: false, isConnected() { return this.connected; }, async close() { this.closed = true; this.connected = false; }, crash() { this.connected = false; } };
+    started.push(b);
+    return /** @type {any} */ (b);
+  };
+  return { start, started };
+}
+
+test('a pool lends its idle browsers again and starts only what it lacks', async () => {
+  const { start, started } = fakes();
+  const pool = new BrowserPool({ start });
+  const two = await pool.take(2);
+  assert.equal(started.length, 2);
+  await pool.give(two);
+  const three = await pool.take(3);
+  assert.equal(started.length, 3, 'the two back, one more started');
+  assert.deepEqual(new Set(three.slice(0, 2)), new Set(two));
+  assert.equal(pool.size, 3);
+  await pool.close();
+  assert.ok(started.every((b) => b.closed), 'every browser closed, lent or not');
+  await assert.rejects(pool.take(1), /cancelled/);
+});
+
+test('a browser that crashed, one that failed a render, and one that drew many pages are not lent again', async () => {
+  const { start, started } = fakes();
+  const pool = new BrowserPool({ start });
+  const [a, b] = await pool.take(2);
+  /** @type {any} */ (a).crash();
+  await pool.give([a, b], { retire: false });
+  assert.equal(pool.size, 1, 'the crashed one dropped');
+  const [again] = await pool.take(1);
+  assert.equal(again, b);
+  await pool.give([again], { retire: true });
+  assert.ok(started[1].closed, 'a render that failed: its browser closed');
+  assert.equal(pool.size, 0);
+  const [c] = await pool.take(1);
+  for (let i = 1; i < PAGES_PER_BROWSER; i++) {
+    await pool.give([c]);
+    assert.equal((await pool.take(1))[0], c);
+  }
+  await pool.give([c]);
+  assert.ok(/** @type {any} */ (c).closed, `closed after ${PAGES_PER_BROWSER} pages`);
+  const [fresh] = await pool.take(1);
+  assert.notEqual(fresh, c);
+  await pool.close();
+});
+
+test('a browser that fails to start gives the others back and says why', async () => {
+  const { start, started } = fakes();
+  let calls = 0;
+  const pool = new BrowserPool({ start: () => (++calls === 2 ? Promise.reject(new Error('no browser')) : start()) });
+  await assert.rejects(pool.take(3), /no browser/);
+  assert.equal(pool.size, 2, 'the two that started are kept');
+  assert.equal((await pool.take(2)).length, 2);
+  assert.equal(started.length, 2, 'and lent again');
+  await pool.close();
+});
+
+test('a render is drawn again in new browsers only when its browser failed it', () => {
+  assert.ok(browserFailed(new Error('page.evaluate: Target page, context or browser has been closed')));
+  assert.ok(browserFailed(new Error('page.evaluate: Target crashed')));
+  assert.ok(browserFailed(new Error('the browser drawing the page closed (it crashed, or the system ended it)')));
+  assert.ok(browserFailed(new Error('frame(1.2) did not finish within 60 s')));
+  assert.ok(browserFailed(new Error('the picture did not come within 30 s')));
+  assert.ok(!browserFailed(new Error('frame(1.2) did not finish within 60 s (the page is still running code: a loop that never ends?)')), 'a loop in the page would loop again');
+  assert.ok(!browserFailed(new Error('frame(1.2) threw: x is not defined')));
+  assert.ok(!browserFailed(new Error('ffmpeg: Unknown encoder')));
+});

packages/openfilm/src/encoders.mjs
@@ -83,6 +83,24 @@ export function proresOptions(encoder, alpha) {
   return { video: ['-c:v', 'prores_ks', '-profile:v', alpha ? '4444' : '3', '-vendor', 'apl0'], pix: alpha ? 'format=yuva444p10le' : 'format=yuv422p10le' };
 }
 
+/* BT.709 said on the frames: an encoder that takes no notice of the color options (the Mac's VideoToolbox writes no
+   primaries or transfer from them, and nothing at all for HEVC with alpha) takes it from the frames */
+export const FRAMES_709 = 'setparams=colorspace=bt709:color_primaries=bt709:color_trc=bt709';
+
+/**
+ * ProRes of the browser's frames, BT.709 throughout: converted to YUV with it (swscale's own default is BT.601; the
+ * Mac's engine takes RGB and converts it itself), said on the frames and to the encoder. Footage converted for an
+ * editor keeps its own colors and takes proresOptions as it is.
+ * @param {string} encoder @param {boolean} alpha
+ */
+export function prores709(encoder, alpha) {
+  const { video, pix } = proresOptions(encoder, alpha);
+  return {
+    video: [...video, '-colorspace', 'bt709', '-color_primaries', 'bt709', '-color_trc', 'bt709', '-color_range', 'tv'],
+    pix: pix === 'format=bgra' ? `${pix},${FRAMES_709}` : `scale=out_color_matrix=bt709:out_range=tv,${pix},${FRAMES_709}`,
+  };
+}
+

+/** @type {Map<string, Promise<{ duration: number, w: number, h: number } | null>>} */
+const mediaFactsOf = new Map();
+/**
+ * A video's or sound's length (s) and picture size (0 for a sound), as ffprobe reads them, once per version of the file;
+ * null when it cannot tell.
+ * @param {string} file
+ */
+export function mediaFacts(file) {
+  let key;
+  try { const stat = statSync(file); key = `${file}\0${stat.size}\0${stat.mtimeMs}`; } catch { return Promise.resolve(null); }
+  if (!mediaFactsOf.has(key)) {
+    mediaFactsOf.set(key, new Promise((done) => {
+      execFile('ffprobe', ['-v', 'error', '-show_entries', 'format=duration:stream=codec_type,width,height', '-of', 'json', file],
+        { timeout: 20_000, windowsHide: true }, (error, stdout) => {
+          if (error) { mediaFactsOf.delete(key); done(null); return; }
+          let said;
+          try { said = JSON.parse(String(stdout)); } catch { /* nothing to read */ }
+          const duration = Number(said?.format?.duration);
+          const video = said?.streams?.find((s) => s.codec_type === 'video');
+          done(duration > 0 ? { duration, w: video?.width ?? 0, h: video?.height ?? 0 } : null);
+        });
+    }));
+  }
+  return /** @type {Promise<{ duration: number, w: number, h: number } | null>} */ (mediaFactsOf.get(key));
+}

+/** What a part drawn opaque (render's `clear`) stops with: a pixel that shows through, or alpha that could not be read. @param {string} where */
+const showsThrough = (where) => Object.assign(new Error(`a pixel shows through ${where}`), { code: 'CLEAR' });
+
+/**
+ * Each frame's least alpha, read as the frames are drawn: an ffmpeg beside the encoder takes the same pictures (a
+ * picture with nothing clear comes from the browser as a PNG with no alpha, read as opaque). It starts at the first
+ * picture (no probing: the stream is known), decodes one at a time (threads would hold a dozen back) and says each
+ * frame's at once (`direct`: not held for a buffer to fill), so a render stops a few frames after a pixel shows
+ * through. `clear` turns true at that pixel; `read` counts the frames read; `ended`, its exit code.
+ */
+function alphaWatch() {
+  const proc = spawn('ffmpeg', ['-hide_banner', '-nostdin', '-loglevel', 'error', '-probesize', '32', '-analyzeduration', '0', '-threads', '1', '-f', 'image2pipe', '-c:v', 'png', '-i', '-',
+    '-vf', 'format=rgba,alphaextract,format=gray,signalstats,metadata=print:key=lavfi.signalstats.YMIN:file=-:direct=1', '-f', 'null', '-'], { stdio: ['pipe', 'pipe', 'ignore'], windowsHide: true });
+  proc.stdin.on('error', () => {});
+  const watch = { proc, read: 0, clear: false, ended: new Promise((done) => { proc.on('error', () => done(-1)); proc.on('close', (code) => done(code)); }) };
+  let rest = '';
+  proc.stdout.on('data', (d) => {
+    const lines = (rest + d).split('\n');
+    rest = lines.pop() ?? '';
+    for (const line of lines) {
+      const m = /YMIN=(\d+)/.exec(line);
+      if (!m) continue;
+      watch.read++;
+      if (Number(m[1]) < 255) watch.clear = true;
+    }
+  });
+  return watch;
+}
+

       for (let k = 0; k < samples; k++) {
         const t = (f + (samples > 1 ? (k / samples - 0.5) * shutter : 0)) / fps;
         await film.seek(Math.min(Math.max(0, t), duration - 1e-4));
         const frame = await film.capture(still === 'png' ? { format: 'png' } : { format: 'jpeg', quality: 95 });
         await Promise.race([write(enc.proc.stdin, frame), ended]);
+        if (watch) await Promise.race([write(watch.proc.stdin, frame), unwatched]);
       }
       onFrame();
     }
   } finally {
     signal?.removeEventListener('abort', stop);
     enc.proc.stdin.end();
+    watch?.proc.stdin.end();
   }
   await enc.done;
+  /* opaque only when every frame was read and none let anything through */
+  if (watch) {
+    const code = await watch.ended;
+    if (watch.clear) throw showsThrough('in its last frames');
+    if (code !== 0 || watch.read !== (b - a) * samples) throw showsThrough(`(its alpha could not be read: ${watch.read} of ${(b - a) * samples} frames, exit ${code})`);
+  }
 }

+/** @type {Map<string, Promise<'made' | 'failed' | 'cancelled'>>} the copies being made in this process, by their path */
+const making = new Map();
+
+/**
+ * `file`'s copy written to `out`, under a name of its own until it is whole: another process making the same copy
+ * never writes the same file, and the one renamed last is kept (the same picture). One that cannot replace a copy
+ * already there (Windows, while a browser reads it) uses that copy.
+ * @param {string} file @param {string} out @param {{ fps: number, w: number, h: number, alpha: boolean }} need @param {AbortSignal} [signal]
+ * @returns {Promise<'made' | 'failed' | 'cancelled'>}
+ */
+async function makeCopy(file, out, need, signal) {
+  if (signal?.aborted) return 'cancelled';
+  const gop = Math.max(1, Math.round(need.fps * COPY_GOP_S));
+  const part = `${out}.part-${process.pid}-${randomBytes(4).toString('hex')}${extname(out)}`;
+  /* all but lossless: about 19 Mbps at 1080p and 30 fps; the chip's encoder keeps the source's color tags only
+     when told them */
+  const bits = Math.min(120e6, Math.max(4e6, ((need.w * need.h) || 1920 * 1080) * need.fps * 0.3));
+  /* decoded and written on the graphics chip when it can (encoders.mjs), else on the processor */
+  const make = async (/** @type {string | null} */ encoder, /** @type {string[]} */ decoding) => {
+    /* the same frames at the same times (passthrough: no frame is added or dropped), the picture only */
+    const run = ffmpeg([...decoding, '-i', file, '-map', '0:v:0', '-an', '-sn', '-dn', '-fps_mode', 'passthrough',
+      ...(need.alpha ? ['-c:v', 'libvpx-vp9', '-pix_fmt', 'yuva420p', '-b:v', '0', '-crf', '18', '-row-mt', '1', '-deadline', 'realtime', '-cpu-used', '8',
+        '-g', String(gop), '-keyint_min', String(gop), '-auto-alt-ref', '0']
+        : encoder ? ['-pix_fmt', 'nv12', ...hardwareOptions(encoder, bits, { gop }), ...h264ColorTags(await colorTagsOf(file))]
+          : ['-c:v', 'libx264', '-preset', 'superfast', '-crf', '16', '-g', String(gop), '-keyint_min', String(gop), '-pix_fmt', 'yuv420p']), part]);
+    /* its whole tree: an ffmpeg that is a shim (Windows' package managers) runs the real one as its child */
+    const stop = () => kill(run.proc);
+    signal?.addEventListener('abort', stop, { once: true });
+    try { await run.done; } finally { signal?.removeEventListener('abort', stop); }
+  };
+  /* a part that cannot be removed (Windows, a moment after its encoder ends) stays in the cache folder: it never fails
+     the render that asked for the copy */
+  const drop = () => { try { rmSync(part, { force: true }); } catch { /* left */ } };
+  try {
+    await onTheChip(need.alpha ? null : await hardwareEncoder('h264', await encoders()), make, signal);
+  } catch {
+    drop();
+    return signal?.aborted ? 'cancelled' : 'failed';
+  }
+  /* a file a virus scanner or the indexer holds for a moment refuses to move on Windows (EPERM, EACCES, EBUSY): tried
+     again for a few seconds before the copy counts as not made */
+  for (let i = 1; ; i++) {
+    try {
+      renameSync(part, out);
+      return 'made';
+    } catch (e) {
+      if (existsSync(out)) { drop(); return 'made'; }
+      if (i >= 12 || !['EPERM', 'EACCES', 'EBUSY'].includes(/** @type {NodeJS.ErrnoException} */ (e).code ?? '')) { drop(); return 'failed'; }
+      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, i * 100));
+    }
+  }
+}

+/**

+function hostFacts(href) {
+  const src = new URL(href);
+  if (src.origin !== location.origin) return Promise.resolve(null);
+  /* the file from the film's folder, `../` and all (an edit kept apart from its project names its files so) */
+  const here = new URL('.', location.href).pathname.split('/').slice(0, -1);

+/** A seek done: its frame decoded (`seeked`, or the file's first data), or after a time limit. */
+const sought = (v) => new Promise((done) => {

+/** Follow the frames a clip's video puts on screen (its frame callback): `c.presented`, the last one's time. */
+function follow(c) {
+  const v = c.el;
+  if (c.following || typeof v.requestVideoFrameCallback !== 'function') return;
+  c.following = true;
+  const note = (/** @type {number} */ _now, /** @type {{ mediaTime: number }} */ meta) => {
+    c.presented = meta.mediaTime;
+    c.onPresented?.();
+    v.requestVideoFrameCallback(note);
+  };
+  v.requestVideoFrameCallback(note);
+}

+/**
+ * A still moment's frame made sure of on screen while the person stays there: a frame decoded may never be presented
+ * (Chromium, see showVideoAt), and the picture keeps an older one, frames behind what an export draws at the same t.
+ * Not on screen in a moment, it is sought again, half a millisecond on (the same frame), a few times. A scrub that
+ * asks for another moment, or the film playing, leaves it; so does a video whose frames' length the host cannot say.
+ */
+async function onScreen(c, want) {
+  const v = c.el;
+  const frame = await frameOf(c);
+  if (!frame || !c.following) return;
+  /* the frame a seek to want lands in, as showVideoAt tells it */
+  const right = () => c.presented != null && c.presented <= want + 0.002 && want + 0.001 - c.presented < frame * 1.25;
+  const still = () => c.lastWant === want && v.paused && !v.seeking;
+  for (let tries = 1; ; tries++) {
+    if (right() || !still()) return;
+    const shown = await new Promise((done) => {
+      const timer = setTimeout(() => done(false), PRESENT_MS);
+      c.onPresented = () => { if (right()) { clearTimeout(timer); done(true); } };
+    });
+    c.onPresented = undefined;
+    if (shown || tries >= SEEK_TRIES || !still()) return;
+    v.currentTime = want + tries * 0.0005;
+    await sought(v);
+  }
 }

+      await window.__filmEditor.watch(3.25);
+      /* the editor is told the moment is drawn at once: the scrub goes on as fast as the decoder */
+      const told = performance.now() - t0;
+      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 2500));

+    });
+    assert.ok(seen.swallowed, 'the frame was presented, and not said to be');

+/**
+ * A rename that rides out Windows: a file that a virus scanner, the search indexer or an editor watching its folder holds
+ * for a moment refuses to move (EPERM, EACCES, EBUSY), and is let go a moment later. Tried again for a few seconds, as
+ * tools on Windows do; any other error, or a hold that lasts, is the error.
+ * @param {string} from @param {string} to
+ */
+export async function renameSafely(from, to) {
+  for (let i = 1; ; i++) {
+    try {
+      await rename(from, to);
+      return;
+    } catch (e) {
+      if (i >= 12 || !['EPERM', 'EACCES', 'EBUSY'].includes(/** @type {NodeJS.ErrnoException} */ (e).code ?? '')) throw e;
+      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, i * 100));
+    }
+  }

+/** Whether a process is running (one this user may not signal is). @param {number} pid */
+function alive(pid) {

+/**
+ * What exports Studio could not finish left in `dir` (it was ended partway: forced to quit, crashed, the computer
+ * shut down): each entry `pattern` names whose process (its first group) has ended.
+ * @param {string} dir @param {RegExp} pattern
+ */
+export async function sweep(dir, pattern) {
+  const names = await readdir(dir).catch(() => /** @type {string[]} */ ([]));
+  await Promise.all(names.map((name) => {

+const execFileP = promisify(execFile);
+
+/**
+ * How a draft's page with alpha is drawn before its pictures are thinned (keepChanges writes them at ffmpeg's own PNG
+ * settings): PNG at zlib's fastest with each pixel kept as its difference from the one on its left. The same pixels for
+ * an eighth of the processor: ffmpeg's own PNG settings take about 0.4 s of processor time per 1080p frame, more than
+ * drawing it.
+ */
+const QUICK_PNG = { video: ['-c:v', 'png', '-compression_level', '1', '-pred', 'sub'], pix: 'format=rgba' };
+
+/**
+ * A PNG video with each picture kept once: a frame that repeats the one before is dropped and the one before lasts
+ * until the next (the timestamps say so), so a page that holds still is one picture, not a second's worth. A repeat is
+ * found by its bytes: one encoder at one setting writes the same picture the same way, so a frame a pixel apart is
+ * never taken for one; the frames kept are written again as PNG at ffmpeg's own settings, the same pixels. The last
+ * frame stays, so the length does too; a video that changes in most of its frames keeps every frame, written again the
+ * same way (it may have been drawn as a quick PNG).
+ * @param {string} file @param {AbortSignal} signal
+ */
+export async function keepChanges(file, signal) {
+  const { stdout } = await execFileP('ffmpeg', ['-hide_banner', '-nostdin', '-loglevel', 'error', '-i', file, '-map', '0:v:0', '-c', 'copy', '-f', 'framemd5', '-'],
+    { maxBuffer: 256 << 20, signal, windowsHide: true });
+  const sums = String(stdout).split('\n').filter((l) => l && !l.startsWith('#')).map((l) => l.split(',').pop()?.trim());
+  const kept = sums.flatMap((sum, i) => (i === 0 || i === sums.length - 1 || sum !== sums[i - 1] ? [i] : []));
+  /* frames kept one after another are one term; the terms are added as a balanced tree, since ffmpeg parses no more
+     than 100 in a row */
+  /** @type {[number, number][]} */
+  const runs = [];
+  for (const i of kept) { const last = runs.at(-1); if (last && last[1] === i - 1) last[1] = i; else runs.push([i, i]); }
+  /** @type {(part: [number, number][]) => string} */
+  const sum = (part) => (part.length === 1 ? (part[0][0] === part[0][1] ? `eq(n,${part[0][0]})` : `between(n,${part[0][0]},${part[0][1]})`)
+    : `(${sum(part.slice(0, part.length >> 1))}+${sum(part.slice(part.length >> 1))})`);
+  const part = `${file}.part.mov`;
+  const all = kept.length > sums.length / 2;
+  const graph = all ? '' : `[0:v:0]select='${sum(runs)}'[v]`;
+  /* a long one goes in a file: Windows takes a command of no more than 32,767 characters, and a long page that steps
+     every few frames keeps thousands of runs */
+  const script = graph.length > COMMAND_ROOM ? `${part}.filter` : null;
+  if (script) await writeFile(script, graph, 'utf8');
+  try {
+    const select = all ? ['-map', '0:v:0'] : [...(script ? [await filterScriptOption(), script] : ['-filter_complex', graph]), '-map', '[v]', '-fps_mode', 'vfr'];
+    await run(['-i', file, ...select, '-c:v', 'png', part], signal);
+  } finally {
+    if (script) await tidy(script);
+  }
+  await renameSafely(part, file);
+}

+/**
+ * Renders in one process of their own (render-worker.mjs `--pool`) that share its browsers: an editing project's
+ * pages, drawn several at once and page after page, start a browser once rather than for each page, and one whose
+ * browser crashed or stopped answering is drawn again in new ones. `render` takes render's arguments and gives its
+ * result, as the one-shot render does; `close` ends the process and every browser (after the last page, or on cancel).
+ * A process that dies fails every render still in it.
+ */
+function drawer() {
+  const child = fork(fileURLToPath(new URL('./render-worker.mjs', import.meta.url)), ['--pool'], { stdio: ['ignore', 'inherit', 'pipe', 'ipc'] });
+  below(child);
+  let said = '';
+  child.stderr?.on('data', (d) => { said = (said + d).slice(-4000); process.stderr.write(d); });
+  /** @type {Map<string, { onProgress?: (done: number, total: number) => void, done: (r: any) => void, fail: (e: Error) => void, signal?: AbortSignal }>} */
+  const jobs = new Map();
+  /** @type {Error | null} */
+  let gone = null;
+  let next = 0;
+  child.on('message', (/** @type {any} */ m) => {
+    const job = jobs.get(m?.id);
+    if (!job) return;
+    if (m.type === 'progress') job.onProgress?.(m.done, m.total);
+    else if (m.type === 'done' || m.type === 'failed') {
+      jobs.delete(m.id);
+      if (m.type === 'done') job.done(m.result);
+      else job.fail(new Error(job.signal?.aborted ? 'cancelled' : m.error));
+    }
+  });
+  const end = (/** @type {Error} */ e) => {
+    gone ??= e;
+    for (const [id, job] of jobs) { jobs.delete(id); job.fail(job.signal?.aborted ? new Error('cancelled') : e); }
+  };
+  child.on('error', end);
+  child.on('exit', (code, sig) => {
+    const last = said.trim().split('\n').filter((l) => l.trim() && !/^\s+at /.test(l) && !/drawn again in new browsers$/.test(l)).pop();
+    end(new Error(`the renderer stopped (${sig ?? `exit ${code}`})${last ? `: ${last.slice(0, 300)}` : ''}`));
+  });
+  return {
+    /** @param {string[]} args @param {Record<string, any>} opts @returns {Promise<{ out: string, encoder: string, clear?: boolean, lines: string[] }>} */
+    render(args, opts) {
+      const { signal, onProgress, ...rest } = opts;
+      return new Promise((done, fail) => {
+        if (signal?.aborted) return fail(new Error('cancelled'));
+        if (gone) return fail(gone);
+        const id = String(++next);
+        const cancel = () => { if (child.connected) child.send({ type: 'cancel', id }); };
+        signal?.addEventListener('abort', cancel, { once: true });
+        const off = () => signal?.removeEventListener('abort', cancel);
+        jobs.set(id, { onProgress, signal, done: (r) => { off(); done(r); }, fail: (e) => { off(); fail(e); } });
+        child.send({ type: 'render', id, args, opts: rest });
+      });
+    },
+    /** Ends the process: what still draws stops, the browsers close; ended outright if it has not after a few seconds. */
+    async close() {
+      if (child.exitCode != null || child.signalCode != null) return;
+      const exited = new Promise((r) => child.once('exit', r));
+      if (child.connected) child.send({ type: 'close' });
+      /* its browsers and encoders with it: on Windows ending a process leaves its children running */
+      const kill = setTimeout(() => killProcess(child), 6000);
+      await exited;
+      clearTimeout(kill);
+    },
+  };
+}
+
+/** How many frames a file of an editing project is drawn with: a page's or a still's, 0 for anything else. @param {import('./nle.mjs').NleFile} file */
+const framesOf = (file) => (file.job.kind === 'render' ? file.job.frames : 0);
+
+/**
+ * How many of the `free` browsers a page of `frames` draws with, when `left` frames are still to be drawn (its own
+ * included): its share by its frames, at least one, and no more than it would get alone (`alone`). Pages of a film each
+ * draw with one, several at once, so no browser is started for a few frames; a long page gets more.
+ * @param {number} free @param {number} frames @param {number} left @param {number} alone
+ */
+export const shareOf = (free, frames, left, alone) => Math.max(1, Math.min(free, alone, Math.ceil((free * frames) / Math.max(1, left))));
+
 /** A number as render's range takes it: plain digits, no exponent. @param {number} n */
 const num = (n) => String(Number(n.toFixed(3)));

+      join(cache('seek'), `0123abcd.mp4.part-${pid}-0123abcd.mp4`)];

+    const theirs = [join(out, '.Notes.partial'), join(out, 'Old.partial-1-0123456789ab.mp4'), join(cache('export'), 'nle-mine')];

+    live.kill();

+  const dir = join(scratch, 'Opaque');

+    const progress = seen.filter((j) => j.id === done.id && j.status === 'running').map((j) => j.progress);

+/** A film of three pages long enough to be ended partway, for a draft. */
+function threePages(name) {
+  const dir = join(scratch, name);
+  mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
+  const page = (hue) => `<!doctype html><body style="margin:0;width:320px;height:180px"><script>window.film={duration:4,width:320,height:180,frame(t){
+document.body.style.background='hsl('+Math.round(${hue}+t*60)+',70%,40%)'}}</script></body>`;
+  for (const [i, id] of ['a', 'b', 'c'].entries()) writeFileSync(join(dir, `${id}.html`), page(i * 100));
+  writeFileSync(join(dir, 'film.html'), filmHtml({ stage: { w: 320, h: 180 }, tracks: [{ clips: [{ src: 'a.html', id: 'a' }, { src: 'b.html', id: 'b', at: 4 }, { src: 'c.html', id: 'c', at: 8 }] }] }));
+  const drafts = join(scratch, `${name} drafts`);
+  mkdirSync(drafts, { recursive: true });
+  return { dir, drafts, exports: createExports({ captions: subtitles, drafts: (app) => (app === 'jianying' ? drafts : null) }) };
+}
+/** The processes started by `parent` whose command matches `pattern`. */
+const children = (parent, pattern) => spawnSync('pgrep', ['-P', String(parent), '-f', pattern], { encoding: 'utf8' }).stdout.trim().split('\n').filter(Boolean).map(Number);

+test('剪映: the pages\' browsers, ended partway as a crash would end them, are started again and the draft is whole', { ...ffmpeg, timeout: 240_000, skip: ffmpeg.skip || (process.platform === 'win32' && 'no pgrep') }, async () => {
+  const { dir, exports } = threePages('Crashing browsers');
+  const job = exports.start(project('Crashing browsers', dir), { kind: 'nle', format: 'jianying', fps: 30, subtitles: false });
+  await until(exports, job.id, (j) => ['done', 'failed', 'cancelled'].includes(j.status) || (j.framesDone ?? 0) > 20);
+  /* every browser the pages are drawing in, ended at once */
+  const [worker] = children(process.pid, 'render-worker');
+  assert.ok(worker, 'the pages draw in a process of their own');
+  const browsers = children(worker, 'chrom');
+  assert.ok(browsers.length, 'with browsers of its own');
+  for (const pid of browsers) process.kill(pid, 'SIGKILL');
+  const done = await ended(exports, job);
+  assert.equal(done.status, 'done', done.error);
+  for (const id of ['a', 'b', 'c']) assert.equal(frameCount(join(dirname(done.out), 'media', `${id}.mov`)), 120, `${id}: every frame`);

+  const { dir, drafts, exports } = threePages('Cancelled draft');

+  await until(exports, job.id, (j) => ['done', 'failed', 'cancelled'].includes(j.status) || (j.framesDone ?? 0) > 20);
+  const [worker] = children(process.pid, 'render-worker');
+  assert.ok(worker);
+  const asked = Date.now();
+  exports.cancel(job.id);
+  const done = await ended(exports, job);
+  assert.equal(done.status, 'cancelled');
+  assert.ok(Date.now() - asked < 8000, `stopped in ${Date.now() - asked} ms`);
+  assert.deepEqual(readdirSync(drafts), [], 'no draft, and no part of one');
+  for (let i = 0; i < 100 && children(process.pid, 'render-worker').length; i++) await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 50));
+  assert.deepEqual(children(process.pid, 'render-worker'), [], 'its process and browsers gone');
+});

+test('an editing project\'s pages share the browsers by their frames', () => {
+  /* ten pages of 150 frames, three browsers: one each, three pages at once */
+  assert.equal(shareOf(3, 150, 1500, 2), 1);
+  /* the last page alone: as many as it would get on its own */
+  assert.equal(shareOf(3, 150, 150, 2), 2);
+  /* a 900-frame page among four of 150: two of three, the short ones share the third */
+  assert.equal(shareOf(3, 900, 1500, 3), 2);
+  assert.equal(shareOf(1, 150, 600, 2), 1);
+  /* never more than are free */
+  assert.equal(shareOf(1, 900, 900, 3), 1);
+});

+test('a PNG video keeps each picture once, for as long as it shows: a pixel apart is not a repeat', ffmpeg, async () => {
+  const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'of-keep-'));
+  try {
+    const file = join(dir, 'page.mov');
+    /* 30 clear frames; in frames 10 to 19 one pixel is a step off clear (red and alpha 1 of 255) */
+    execFileSync('ffmpeg', ['-v', 'error', '-f', 'lavfi', '-i', 'color=c=black@0:s=16x16:r=30:d=1,format=rgba,drawbox=x=3:y=3:w=1:h=1:color=0x01000001:t=fill:replace=1:enable=between(n\\,10\\,19)', '-c:v', 'png', file]);
+    const decoded = (f) => execFileSync('ffmpeg', ['-v', 'error', '-i', f, '-vf', 'format=rgba', '-fps_mode', 'cfr', '-r', '30', '-f', 'framemd5', '-']).toString().split('\n').filter((l) => l && !l.startsWith('#')).map((l) => l.split(',').pop());
+    const before = decoded(file);
+    await keepChanges(file, new AbortController().signal);
+    assert.equal(frameCount(file), 4, 'the first, the step, the step back, the last');
+    assert.deepEqual(decoded(file), before, 'every frame the same pixels at the same time');
+    /* a picture that changes every third frame: far more pictures kept than ffmpeg takes in one sum */
+    const busy = join(dir, 'busy.mov');
+    execFileSync('ffmpeg', ['-v', 'error', '-f', 'lavfi', '-i', 'testsrc2=s=32x32:r=10:d=40,fps=30,format=rgba', '-c:v', 'png', busy]);
+    const busyBefore = decoded(busy);
+    await keepChanges(busy, new AbortController().signal);
+    const pictures = busyBefore.filter((sum, i) => i === 0 || i === busyBefore.length - 1 || sum !== busyBefore[i - 1]).length;
+    assert.ok(pictures > 300, `${pictures} pictures`);
+    assert.equal(frameCount(busy), pictures, 'each picture once, and the last frame');
+    assert.deepEqual(decoded(busy), busyBefore);
+    /* a long page that steps every few frames: more runs than a command line holds on Windows, the sum read from a file */
+    const steps = join(dir, 'steps.mov');
+    execFileSync('ffmpeg', ['-v', 'error', '-f', 'lavfi', '-i', 'testsrc2=s=32x32:r=10:d=240,fps=30,format=rgba', '-c:v', 'png', steps]);
+    const stepsBefore = decoded(steps);
+    const stepPictures = stepsBefore.filter((sum, i) => i === 0 || i === stepsBefore.length - 1 || sum !== stepsBefore[i - 1]).length;
+    assert.ok(stepPictures > 2000, `${stepPictures} pictures`);
+    await keepChanges(steps, new AbortController().signal);
+    assert.equal(frameCount(steps), stepPictures);
+    assert.deepEqual(decoded(steps), stepsBefore);
+    assert.deepEqual(readdirSync(dir).filter((n) => n.endsWith('.filter')), [], 'its sum file gone');
+    /* drawn as a quick PNG and changing in every frame: every frame kept, written again at ffmpeg's own settings */
+    const quick = join(dir, 'quick.mov');
+    const own = join(dir, 'own.mov');
+    const moving = ['-v', 'error', '-f', 'lavfi', '-i', 'testsrc2=s=64x36:r=30:d=1,format=rgba'];
+    execFileSync('ffmpeg', [...moving, '-c:v', 'png', '-compression_level', '1', '-pred', 'sub', quick]);
+    execFileSync('ffmpeg', [...moving, '-c:v', 'png', own]);
+    const quickBefore = decoded(quick);
+    await keepChanges(quick, new AbortController().signal);
+    assert.equal(frameCount(quick), 30);
+    assert.deepEqual(decoded(quick), quickBefore, 'the same pixels at the same time');
+    const packets = (f) => execFileSync('ffmpeg', ['-v', 'error', '-i', f, '-map', '0:v:0', '-c', 'copy', '-f', 'framemd5', '-']).toString().split('\n').filter((l) => l && !l.startsWith('#')).map((l) => l.split(',').pop());
+    assert.deepEqual(packets(quick), packets(own), 'written as ffmpeg writes a PNG');
+  } finally {
+    rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
+  }
+});

+test('burned-in bilingual subtitles: the original and its translation, each once', ffmpeg, async () => {
+  /* how much of the bottom half of a picture (the title's own text is at the top) is near-white: the subtitles' text */
+  const lit = (file) => {
+    const [w, h] = probe(file, 'stream=width,height').split(',').map(Number);
+    const rgb = execFileSync('ffmpeg', ['-v', 'error', '-i', file, '-frames:v', '1', '-f', 'rawvideo', '-pix_fmt', 'rgb24', '-']);
+    let n = 0;
+    for (let i = Math.floor(h / 2) * w; i < w * h; i++) if (Math.min(rgb[i * 3], rgb[i * 3 + 1], rgb[i * 3 + 2]) > 200) n++;
+    return n;
+  };
+  const cues = [{ startMs: 0, durMs: 900, text: 'Hello there', alt: { fr: 'Bonjour' } }];
+  /* the original alone, the translation alone, then both, each in the same type: the two lines together are as much
+     text as the two alone, however a font draws them; the translation twice would be a whole translation more */
+  const burned = async (style, name) => {
+    const exports = createExports({ captions: async () => ({ cues, style: { ...parseFilmSubtitleStyle(null), ...style } }) });
+    const done = await ended(exports, exports.start(project(name), { kind: 'poster', at: 0.25, shortEdge: 360, burnSubtitles: true }));
+    assert.equal(done.status, 'done', done.error);
+    return lit(done.out);
+  };
+  const original = await burned({}, 'Original');
+  const translation = await burned({ language: 'fr', bilingual: false }, 'Translation');
+  const both = await burned({ language: 'fr', bilingual: true }, 'Bilingual');
+  assert.ok(original > 0 && translation > 0);
+  assert.ok(Math.abs(both - (original + translation)) < translation / 2, `both ${both}, original ${original}, translation ${translation}`);
+});

+import { test } from 'node:test';
+import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
+import { existsSync, mkdirSync, mkdtempSync, readFileSync, readdirSync, rmSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
+import fsPromises from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { syncBuiltinESMExports } from 'node:module';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import { dirname, join } from 'node:path';
+import { filmHtml, readFilmFile } from '../../src/film-doc.mjs';
+import { filmCaptions } from './captions.mjs';
+import { FileError, moveFile } from './files.mjs';
+import { CUT_NOTE, transcriptPath } from './transcripts.mjs';
+
+const MEDIA = Buffer.from('the original media bytes');
+const ORIGINAL = Buffer.from(`\ufeffWEBVTT\r\n\r\nNOTE Keep this production note exactly.\r\n\r\n${CUT_NOTE}\r\n\r\ncue-1\r\n00:00:00.125 --> 00:00:01.875 align:start\r\n<v Ann><00:00:00.125>Hello <00:00:00.875>world.\r\n`);
+const FRENCH = Buffer.from('WEBVTT\n\nNOTE French translation.\n\n00:00:00.125 --> 00:00:01.875\nBonjour le monde.\n');
+const CHINESE = Buffer.from('WEBVTT\r\n\r\n00:00:00.125 --> 00:00:01.875\r\n你好，世界。\r\n');
+const TRANSCRIPTS = [['', ORIGINAL], ['fr', FRENCH], ['zh-CN', CHINESE]];
+
+function put(root, rel, bytes) {
+  mkdirSync(dirname(join(root, rel)), { recursive: true });
+  writeFileSync(join(root, rel), bytes);
+}
+
+function project(t, sources = ['assets/voice.wav']) {
+  const root = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'of-files-transcripts-'));
+  t.after(() => rmSync(root, { recursive: true, force: true }));
+  for (const src of sources) put(root, src, MEDIA);
+  put(root, 'film.html', filmHtml({ stage: { w: 320, h: 180 }, tracks: [{
+    clips: sources.map((src, index) => ({ id: `clip-${index}`, src, at: index * 3, time: [0, 2] })),
+  }] }));
+  return root;
+}
+
+function putTranscripts(root, src) {
+  for (const [language, bytes] of TRANSCRIPTS) put(root, transcriptPath(src, language || undefined), bytes);
+}
+
+function assertTranscripts(root, src, present = true) {
+  for (const [language, bytes] of TRANSCRIPTS) {
+    const path = join(root, transcriptPath(src, language || undefined));
+    if (present) assert.deepEqual(readFileSync(path), bytes, path);
+    else assert.equal(existsSync(path), false, path);
+  }
+}
+
+function filmSources(root) {
+  return readFilmFile(readFileSync(join(root, 'film.html'), 'utf8')).value.tracks.flatMap((track) => track.clips.map((clip) => clip.src));
+}
+
+async function captions(root) {
+  const value = await filmCaptions(root, { duration: async () => 2 });
+  return { ...value, cues: value.cues.map(({ src, ...cue }) => cue) };
+}
+
+for (const [from, to] of [
+  ['assets/voice.wav', 'assets/narration.wav'],
+  ['assets/voice.mp4', 'assets/cuts/interview.mp4'],
+]) {
+  test(`${from} → ${to} preserves raw transcripts and translated film captions`, async (t) => {
+    const root = project(t, [from]);
+    putTranscripts(root, from);
+    const before = await captions(root);
+    assert.equal(before.cues.length, 1);
+    assert.deepEqual([...before.languages].sort(), ['fr', 'zh-CN']);
+    assert.ok(before.cues[0].alt.fr && before.cues[0].alt['zh-CN']);
+
+    assert.deepEqual(await moveFile(root, from, to), { path: to, clips: 1, pages: 0 });
+
+    assert.equal(existsSync(join(root, from)), false);
+    assert.deepEqual(readFileSync(join(root, to)), MEDIA);
+    assertTranscripts(root, to);
+    assertTranscripts(root, from, false);
+    assert.deepEqual(filmSources(root), [to]);
+    assert.deepEqual(await captions(root), before);
+  });
+}

+test('a case-only media rename keeps canonical subtitles and translated captions', async (t) => {

+  assert.deepEqual(readFileSync(join(root, to)), MEDIA);

+for (const shared of [false, true]) for (const failure of ['sidecar', 'media']) {
+  test(`${failure} failure rolls back ${shared ? 'shared copies' : 'moved sidecars'} before changing the film`, async (t) => {
+    const from = 'assets/voice.mp4', to = 'assets/edited/narration.mp4';
+    const root = project(t, shared ? [from, 'assets/voice.m4a'] : [from]);
+    putTranscripts(root, from);
+    const film = readFileSync(join(root, 'film.html'));
+    const method = failure === 'sidecar' && shared ? 'copyFile' : 'rename';
+    const original = fsPromises[method];
+    const denied = Object.assign(new Error('file is locked'), { code: 'EACCES' });
+    let sidecars = 0, published;
+    const sources = new Set(TRANSCRIPTS.map(([language]) => join(root, transcriptPath(from, language || undefined))));
+    t.mock.method(fsPromises, method, async (source, destination, ...args) => {
+      const fail = failure === 'media' ? source === join(root, from) : sources.has(source) && ++sidecars === 2;
+      if (fail) {
+        published = TRANSCRIPTS.filter(([language]) => existsSync(join(root, transcriptPath(to, language || undefined)))).length;
+        throw denied;
+      }
+      return original(source, destination, ...args);
+    });
+    syncBuiltinESMExports();
+    try {
+      await assert.rejects(moveFile(root, from, to), (error) => error === denied);
+    } finally {
+      t.mock.restoreAll();
+      syncBuiltinESMExports();
+    }
+
+    assert.equal(published, failure === 'sidecar' ? 1 : 3, 'failure happens after sidecars have been published');
+    assert.deepEqual(readFileSync(join(root, from)), MEDIA);
+    assert.equal(existsSync(join(root, to)), false);
+    assertTranscripts(root, from);
+    assertTranscripts(root, to, false);
+    assert.deepEqual(readFileSync(join(root, 'film.html')), film);
+  });

+/** Move media and its sidecars together, refusing every conflict before anything moves. */
+async function moveMedia(/** @type {string} */ root, /** @type {string} */ src, /** @type {string} */ dst) {
+  const stem = basename(src, extname(src)), targetStem = basename(dst, extname(dst));
+  const entries = await readdir(dirname(src), { withFileTypes: true });

+    && ['audio', 'video'].includes(kindOf(entry.name)) && basename(entry.name, extname(entry.name)) === stem);
+  const moves = entries.filter((entry) => entry.isFile() && (entry.name === `${stem}.vtt`
+    || (entry.name.startsWith(`${stem}.`) && /^\.([A-Za-z]{2,3}(?:-[A-Za-z0-9]{2,8})*)\.vtt$/.test(entry.name.slice(stem.length)))))
+    .map((entry) => ({
+      from: inside(root, posix(relative(root, join(dirname(src), entry.name)))),
+      to: inside(root, posix(relative(root, join(dirname(dst), targetStem + entry.name.slice(stem.length))))),
+    })).filter(({ from, to }) => from !== to && !(shared && sameEntry(from, to)));
+  for (const { from, to } of moves) {
+    if (lstatSync(to, { throwIfNoEntry: false }) && !sameEntry(from, to)) {
+      throw new FileError(`${posix(relative(root, to))} is already there`, 409);
+    }
+  }
+  await mkdir(dirname(dst), { recursive: true });
+  /** @type {typeof moves} */
+  const done = [];
+  try {
+    for (const move of moves) {
+      if (shared) await copyFile(move.from, move.to, constants.COPYFILE_EXCL);
+      else await rename(move.from, move.to);
+      done.push(move);
+    }
+    await rename(src, dst);
+  } catch (error) {
+    const failures = [];
+    for (const move of done.reverse()) {
+      try {
+        if (shared) await unlink(move.to);
+        else await rename(move.to, move.from);
+      } catch (restoreError) { failures.push(restoreError); }
+    }
+    if (failures.length) throw new AggregateError([error, ...failures], 'Could not restore the subtitles after the media move failed');
+    throw error;
+  }
+  return shared ? [] : moves.map(({ from, to }) => ({ from: posix(relative(root, from)), to: posix(relative(root, to)) }));
+}
+

+  const draw = async (id, args, opts, signal) => {
+    try {

+        } catch (e) {

+    if (m?.type === 'render') {

+      running.set(m.id, job);
+      job.drawn = draw(m.id, m.args, m.opts, job.abort.signal).finally(() => running.delete(m.id));
+    } else if (m?.type === 'cancel') {

+    } else if (m?.type === 'close') {

+      for (const job of running.values()) job.abort.abort();
+      await Promise.race([Promise.allSettled([...running.values()].map((j) => j.drawn)), new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 4000))]);

+/* A card stays until the person closes it: a finished export is found where it was left, its file one click away.
+   Closed ones stay closed while Studio runs, the project or the page opened again or not (its exports are this

+const closedByProject = new Map<string, Set<string>>();
+const CLOSED_KEY = 'openfilm.exportTray.closed';
+function closedOf(projectId: string): Set<string> {
+  const known = closedByProject.get(projectId);
+  if (known) return known;
+  let ids: string[] = [];
+  try { ids = JSON.parse(sessionStorage.getItem(`${CLOSED_KEY}.${projectId}`) ?? '[]'); } catch { /* nothing kept */ }
+  return new Set(Array.isArray(ids) ? ids.filter((id) => typeof id === 'string') : []);

+/* ───────────── text color: `color`, or the page's own text fill over it ───────────── */
+
+/**
+ * What words are drawn in when their page gives them a fill of its own: `-webkit-text-fill-color` paints the glyphs
+ * over `color` (gold or gradient words often have one).
+ */
+export const TEXT_FILL = '-webkit-text-fill-color';
+
+const ownFill = (fill: string | undefined) => fill != null && fill !== '' && !/^currentcolor$/i.test(fill.trim());
+/** A fill no one sees: words showing what is behind them (a gradient clipped to the text). */
+const seeThrough = (fill: string) => /^transparent$/i.test(fill.trim()) || /^#[0-9a-f]{6}00$/i.test(fill.trim());
+
+/** The color words are drawn in: their own fill when it shows, else `color`. */
+export function textColorOf(read: (prop: string) => string | undefined): string | undefined {
+  const fill = read(TEXT_FILL);
+  return ownFill(fill) && !seeThrough(fill!) ? fill : read('color');
+}
+
+/** Words drawn in `color`: with the page's own fill taken over by it (`currentcolor`), or the new color would not show. */
+export function textColorCss(read: (prop: string) => string | undefined, color: string): Record<string, string> {
+  return ownFill(read(TEXT_FILL)) ? { color, [TEXT_FILL]: 'currentcolor' } : { color };
+}
+
 /* ───────────── inner shadow: an inset box-shadow ───────────── */
 
 /** A shadow cast inside the box, over its fill (an inner shadow). */

packages/openfilm/studio/ui/src/lib/look.test.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,83 @@
+import { test } from 'node:test';
+import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
+import { makeLook, previewSettled, type LookRead, type LookSet } from './look.ts';
+import { MIXED } from './mixed.ts';
+import { TEXT_FILL, textColorCss } from './clip-look.ts';
+
+type Thing = { id: string; css: Record<string, string> };
+const readOf = (t: Thing): LookRead => (prop) => t.css[prop];
+
+/** A look over `things` that records what it is asked to write and to show. */
+function watched(things: Thing[]) {
+  const commits: [string, LookSet][][] = [];
+  const previews: [string, LookSet][][] = [];
+  let reverts = 0;
+  const look = makeLook(things, readOf, {
+    commit: (sets) => commits.push(sets.map(([t, s]) => [t.id, s])),
+    preview: (sets) => previews.push(sets.map(([t, s]) => [t.id, s])),
+    revert: () => { reverts++; },
+  });
+  return { look, commits, previews, reverts: () => reverts };
+}
+
+test('a look tells a value its things agree on, and MIXED when they do not', () => {
+  const a = { id: 'a', css: { opacity: '0.5', color: '#ffffff' } };
+  const b = { id: 'b', css: { opacity: '0.5', color: '#000000' } };
+  const { look } = watched([a, b]);
+  assert.equal(look.n, 2);
+  assert.equal(look.common((read) => read('opacity')), '0.5');
+  assert.equal(look.common((read) => read('color')), MIXED);
+  assert.equal(watched([]).look.common((read) => read('opacity')), MIXED, 'nothing in hand agrees on nothing');
+});
+
+test('a commit writes only what it changes, and only for the things it changes', () => {
+  const a = { id: 'a', css: { opacity: '1' } };
+  const b = { id: 'b', css: { opacity: '0.5' } };
+  const { look, commits } = watched([a, b]);
+  look.commit(() => ({ opacity: '0.5', filter: 'none' }));
+  assert.deepEqual(commits, [[['a', { opacity: '0.5', filter: 'none' }], ['b', { filter: 'none' }]]]);
+  look.commit((read) => ({ opacity: read('opacity') ?? null }));
+  assert.equal(commits.length, 1, 'a no-op is not an undo step');
+});
+
+test('a preview kept by a commit is still written, though the things read as the preview by then', () => {
+  const thing = { id: 'a', css: { opacity: '1' } };
+  const { look, previews } = watched([thing]);
+  look.preview(() => ({ opacity: '0.4' }));
+  assert.deepEqual(previews, [[['a', { opacity: '0.4' }]]]);
+  /* measured again while previewed: the panel makes a new look over what the picture shows */
+  const now = watched([{ id: 'a', css: { opacity: '0.4' } }]);
+  now.look.commit(() => ({ opacity: '0.4' }));
+  assert.deepEqual(now.commits, [[['a', { opacity: '0.4' }]]]);
+});
+
+test('a commit is worked out from before its preview: a text color dragged in the picker keeps taking over the words\' own fill', () => {
+  /* gold words: their page paints them with a fill of their own over their color */
+  const gold = { id: 'head', css: { color: '#b39458', [TEXT_FILL]: '#b39458' } };
+  const { look, previews } = watched([gold]);
+  look.preview((read) => textColorCss(read, '#6b5020'));
+  assert.deepEqual(previews, [[['head', { color: '#6b5020', [TEXT_FILL]: 'currentcolor' }]]]);
+  /* previewed, the words' fill follows their color: measured again, they have no fill of their own */
+  const now = watched([{ id: 'head', css: { color: '#6b5020' } }]);
+  now.look.preview((read) => textColorCss(read, '#402d07'));
+  now.look.commit((read) => textColorCss(read, '#402d07'));
+  assert.deepEqual(now.commits, [[['head', { color: '#402d07', [TEXT_FILL]: 'currentcolor' }]]]);
+});
+
+test('a preview taken back, or painted back by hand, leaves nothing for the next commit to work from', () => {
+  const gold = { id: 'head', css: { color: '#b39458', [TEXT_FILL]: '#b39458' } };
+  const first = watched([gold]);
+  first.look.preview((read) => textColorCss(read, '#6b5020'));
+  first.look.revert();
+  assert.equal(first.reverts(), 1);
+  const plain = watched([{ id: 'head', css: { color: '#ffffff' } }]);
+  plain.look.commit((read) => textColorCss(read, '#ff0000'));
+  assert.deepEqual(plain.commits, [[['head', { color: '#ff0000' }]]], 'reverted: the fill from before is not used');
+
+  const second = watched([gold]);
+  second.look.preview((read) => textColorCss(read, '#6b5020'));
+  previewSettled();
+  const again = watched([{ id: 'head', css: { color: '#ffffff' } }]);
+  again.look.commit((read) => textColorCss(read, '#ff0000'));
+  assert.deepEqual(again.commits, [[['head', { color: '#ff0000' }]]], 'settled: the fill from before is not used');
+});

packages/openfilm/studio/ui/src/lib/look.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,83 @@
+/**
+ * The inspector's look controls over one or several things (layers inside a page, or clips): what each reads of their
+ * CSS, and how an edit is shown (previewed) and written (committed) for each of them.
+ */
+import { MIXED, type Mixed } from './mixed.ts';
+
+/** One thing's CSS property (kebab-case), as it is now. */
+export type LookRead = (prop: string) => string | undefined;
+/** Properties to write: a value, or null — "none of mine" (the panel decides what that leaves). */
+export type LookSet = Record<string, string | null>;
+/** What a control does to one thing, from that thing's own values. */
+export type LookEdit = (read: LookRead) => LookSet;
+
+export interface Look {
+  /** How many things it is. */
+  n: number;
+  /** What `fn` makes of every thing's values, when they agree; MIXED when they do not. */
+  common<V>(fn: (read: LookRead) => V): V | Mixed;
+  /** Shows an edit on the picture, nothing written. */
+  preview(edit: LookEdit): void;
+  /** Writes an edit, as one change. */
+  commit(edit: LookEdit): void;
+  /** Takes a preview back off the picture. */
+  revert(): void;
+}
+
+/**
+ * A Look over `targets`: read each through `readOf`, and hand each one's properties to `write` (the panel stores
+ * them). `write` gets only the things an edit changes.
+ */
+export function makeLook<Tg>(
+  targets: readonly Tg[],
+  readOf: (t: Tg) => LookRead,
+  write: {
+    commit: (sets: [Tg, LookSet][]) => void;
+    preview?: (sets: [Tg, LookSet][]) => void;
+    revert?: () => void;
+  },
+): Look {
+  const reads = targets.map(readOf);
+  const sets = (edit: LookEdit, kept?: ReadonlyMap<string, string | undefined>[] | null): [Tg, LookSet][] => targets.flatMap((tg, i) => {
+    const read = reads[i]!;
+    /* what was there before a preview: the layer is measured again while previewed, so by now it reads as the
+       preview. The edit is worked out from it (a preview may have changed what the edit decides by, as text color
+       taking over the words' own fill), and unchanged properties are not written: the agent reads the diff, a no-op
+       would be an undo step, and a kept preview would read as no change */
+    const was = (k: string) => (kept?.[i]?.has(k) ? kept[i]!.get(k) : read(k));
+    const set = edit(was);
+    const changed = Object.entries(set).filter(([k, v]) => (v ?? undefined) !== was(k));
+    return changed.length ? [[tg, Object.fromEntries(changed)] as [Tg, LookSet]] : [];
+  });
+  return {
+    n: targets.length,
+    common(fn) {
+      if (!reads.length) return MIXED;
+      const first = fn(reads[0]!);
+      const key = JSON.stringify(first);
+      return reads.every((r) => JSON.stringify(fn(r)) === key) ? first : MIXED;
+    },
+    /* shown as said, unchanged or not: a layer reads as its kept change while a preview paints over it, so painting
+       back what was there would read as no change */
+    preview(edit) {
+      if (!previewedBefore || previewedBefore.length !== targets.length) previewedBefore = targets.map(() => new Map());
+      const list = targets.flatMap((tg, i) => {
+        const set = edit(reads[i]!);
+        for (const k of Object.keys(set)) if (!previewedBefore![i]!.has(k)) previewedBefore![i]!.set(k, reads[i]!(k));
+        return Object.keys(set).length ? [[tg, set] as [Tg, LookSet]] : [];
+      });
+      write.preview?.(list);
+    },
+    commit(edit) {
+      const list = sets(edit, previewedBefore);
+      previewedBefore = null;
+      if (list.length) write.commit(list);
+    },
+    revert() { previewedBefore = null; write.revert?.(); },
+  };
+}
+

+/** The loudest a clip's volume goes (500%), as the inspector's slider. */
+export const VOLUME_MAX = 5;
+
+/** The volume slider's range in dB: its bottom is silence, its top the loudest a clip goes (500%, +14 dB). */
+export const VOLUME_DB_MIN = -60;
+export const VOLUME_DB_MAX = Math.round(20 * Math.log10(VOLUME_MAX) * 10) / 10;
+
+/** A clip's volume as the slider shows it: dB to the tenth, silence at the bottom. */
+export function volumeToDb(volume: number): number {
+  if (!(volume > 0)) return VOLUME_DB_MIN;
+  const db = Math.round(20 * Math.log10(volume) * 10) / 10;
+  return Math.max(VOLUME_DB_MIN, Math.min(VOLUME_DB_MAX, db));
+}
+
+/** The volume a dB on the slider writes, to the thousandth: silent at its bottom, at most 500%. */
+export function dbToVolume(db: number): number {
+  if (!(db > VOLUME_DB_MIN)) return 0;
+  return Math.round(Math.min(VOLUME_MAX, 10 ** (db / 20)) * 1000) / 1000;
+}
